### Fishing
Enchanted Pufferfish can rarely be fished as a [[Treasure|GREAT CATCH]] or an [[Treasure|OUTSTANDING CATCH]] anywhere in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] (except in [[Galatea]] and [[Jerry's Workshop]]).
### Seal's Treat Bag
16x Enchanted Pufferfish have a 5.5% (1 in 18) chance to be obtained from a [[Seal's Treat Bag]].
## Usage
### [[Crafting]] Recipe

10x Enchanted Sand is a potential reward from the [[Treasure Hunter]]'s buried treasure.

The Enderman Mask is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] received from the [[End Race]].
## Obtaining
### End Race
The Enderman Mask is given by [[Guber]] when the [[End Race]] is completed in under 2 minutes. Afterwards, it can be purchased from [[Rusty]] for 1,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Helmet:
Enderman Mask [[Health]]: +1 [[Defense]]: +1 [[Strength]]: +1 [[Intelligence]]: -99
## Trivia
- If you wear this mask and an item that reduces your [[Mana]] below 1, you can infinitely spam the [[Jingle Bells]] and [[Power Orbs]]. Please note this has no practical use and should only be done as a joke.
- By adding [[Wisdom (Enchantment)|Wisdom V]] to the mask, equipping the mask will allow you to gain [[Mana]].
- By combining this helmet with other items such as the [[Jerry Pet]], it is possible to gain a negative amount of [[Intelligence]].
## History

The Ent Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Woodland Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
An Ent Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] an [[Ent]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Huntraps
An Ent Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Murkwater Depths]] (Underwater) or [[Murkwater Shallows]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
An Ent Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Ent Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Woodland Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Woodland Ruler ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3%–30% more [[Damage]] against [[Woodland]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
An Ent Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Ent Shard has the following description:
- Ents can communicate with all plant life.

# Eternal Flame Ring
Aliases: Flame Ring, Flame ring
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Mob Drops, Crystal Hollows
[Eternal Flame Ring]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: A ring that calls you towards the eternal flame of the pits of lava.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Sell: 10,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ETERNAL_FLAME_RING
Tooltip: A ring that calls you towards the eternal flame of the pits of lava.; UNCOMMON
The Eternal Flame Ring is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] Item obtained from lava fishing mobs. It can be thrown into a specific pool of lava for rewards.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv92]|[[Flaming Worm]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.5%|
|[Lv123]|[[Lava Blaze]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.5%|
|[Lv129]|[[Lava Pigman]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.5%|
## Usage
The Eternal Flame Ring grants rewards when thrown into lava in a specific structure within the [[Magma Fields]]. The structure is shaped like a ring of lava with an island with a chest in the middle. Throwing the Ring into the lava rewards the player with 64 (1 stack) [[Gemstone|Rough Topaz Gemstone]]. Doing so for the first time will also grant the player the [[Achievements|"The Ring" Achievement]].
## Trivia
- The Eternal Flame Ring may reference the One Ring from The Lord of the Rings.
- When the Ring rewarded Powder, the Powder would be dropped as a dye item named "Mithril Powder" or "Gemstone Powder".
## Gallery
- The lava pit required to throw the Ring in
- Top view
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Eternal Flame Ring.|
|Jul 27, 2021|Added the "The Ring" Achievement ([[Achievements]]).|
|Unknown Date|Removed the Powder drops from dropping the Ring into the lava.|
|Jul 8, 2025|0.23.1|Can now be sold to NPCs for 10,000 [[Coins]].|

Farmer Boots are [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Boots]] with stats dependent on the player's [[Farming]] level, similar to the [[Lantern Helmet]]. It requires 40,960 (640 stacks) [[Pumpkin|Pumpkins]] to craft. It is unlocked at [[Pumpkin|Pumpkin IX (9)]].

A Farming Contest Display is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] Cosmetic that shows the player's [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Farming Contest]] stats.
## Obtaining
### Garden Visitors
A Farming Contest Display can be obtained by giving [[Jacob]] a filled [[Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set]] while [[Garden Visitors|Visiting]] [[The Garden]]. The next time he visits, a Farming Contest Display appears as a reward.
## Usage
Placing a Farming Contest Display displays the player's [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Farming Contest]] stats. Right-clicking on the display allows the player to select specific crops to show or pick it back up.
## Trivia
- On May 5th, 2026, 55[[Ranks|[ዞ]]] MotorGorilla claimed that two items from the [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]] update had not been found. Shortly after the claim, he had realized he checked the [[Auction House]] on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network|Alpha Network]] and was unaware that the Farming Contest Display was found.

- The Farming Talisman is bugged and is unlikely to work when there are multiple players in a same server. This is inconsistent as it can work with 3 players but not 2.

Item ID: FEASTFUNGUS
Tooltip: Dropped rarely from Mushroom during the Harvest Feast event.; Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
Feastfungus is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item dropped during the [[Harvest Feast]] used to craft various items.
## Obtaining
### Farming
Feastfungus has a 0.006% (1 in 18,000 crops) chance to drop when breaking mature [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]] and [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]], a 15% chance to drop from a [[Slug]] and a 30% chance to drop from a [[Field Mouse]] while the [[Harvest Feast]] is ongoing and the crop is in season.
[[Overbloom]] is used to increase the drop chance.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[["Fragranced" Brown Mushroom "Paste"]]|None|1x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] [[Feastfungus]] 2x Feastfungus|Recipe: Feastfungus ×2 + [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]] → [["Fragranced" Brown Mushroom "Paste"]]|
|[[Fungal Tip Jar]]|None|96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] [[Feastfungus]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) Feastfungus|Recipe: Feastfungus x32 ×4 + [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]] x32 ×3 → [[Fungal Tip Jar]]|
|[[Feast Burger with a Side of Deepfries]]|None|[[Feastfungus]] 12x Feastfungus 12x [[Deepfries]] 12x [[Cornucopia]] 24x [[Enchanted Hay Bale]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] x6 ×4 + [[Deepfries]] x6 ×2 + Feastfungus x6 ×2 + [[Cornucopia]] x12 → [[Feast Burger with a Side of Deepfries]]|

The Field Mouse Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Pest Luck]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Field Mouse Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Field Mouse]]. Since it is an [[Elusive]] mob, it is guaranteed to be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] and drop one.
### Fusion
A Field Mouse Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Field Mouse Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Pest Luck]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Pest Luck ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Overbloom]] on Pest ([[Pest (Mob Type)]]).
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Field Mouse Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Field Mouse Shard has the following description:
- Their keen senses allow them to detect food sources from great distances, making them persistent and hard to eradicate.

# Fiesta Flask
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, More info needed, Aura's Mayorship, Events, Mining Fiesta, Mining
[Fiesta Flask]
Type: Consumable Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

[Shop]
Merchants: [[Brynmor]]
Buy: 2,500,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FIESTA_FLASK
Tooltip: Consumable; Consume while Mayor Cole's Mining Fiesta perk is active to gain the following effects:; • +25%  Mining Fortune • +100% chance of finding Refined Minerals.; • +100% chance of finding Glossy Gemstones.; Right-click to consume!; * Soulbound * LEGENDARY
A Fiesta Flask is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] consumable.
## Obtaining
A Fiesta Flask can be purchased from [[Brynmor]] in the [[Dwarven Tavern]] while [[Cole]]'s [[Mining Fiesta]] perk is active. It costs 2,500,000 [[Coins]] with a stock of 5 per mayorship.
## Usage
Consumable:
Fiesta Flask
[Effects]
[Consume while Mayor Cole Cole's Mining Fiesta perk is active to gain the following effects: +25% Mining Fortune +100% chance of finding Refined Mineral Refined Minerals (Refined Mineral) +100% chance of finding Glossy Gemstone Glossy Gemstones (Glossy Gemstone) Duration: 60m]
- Only one can be consumed at a time. Stacks additively with the [[Mining Fiesta]] perk. Despite its description, it can be bought and used while [[Cole]] is elected as a [[Mayor Election|Minister]].
- If the effect remains on the player without a [[Mining Fiesta]] active, the effect's duration will not go down.
- Upon consuming it, the player will receive the ???[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) effect granting +25% [[Mining Fortune]] and an additional +100% chance to find [[Refined Mineral|Refined Minerals]] and [[Glossy Gemstone|Glossy Gemstones]] for 60 minutes.
- The [[Attributes|Refined Luck]] ( [[Stoneworm Shard]]) and [[Attributes|Glossy Fortune]] ( [[Scatha Shard]]) attributes will additionally have their effects be doubled if the player has a Fiesta Flask active.
## Trivia
- The Fiesta Flask was added by 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] zj2 as part of the [[Minister Election|Minister Elections]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Aug 25, 2026|Added Fiesta Flask.|

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Oct 14, 2026|Doubled the effects of Refined Luck ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Stoneworm Shard]]) and Glossy Fortune ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Scatha Shard]]) while a [[Fiesta Flask]] Fiesta Flask is active.|

Tooltip: Bring 3,000 of these to Carrolyn in Scarleton on the Crimson Isle to permanently gain +5 Health and +12 Wheat Fortune.; UNCOMMON
Fine Flour is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
Completing visitor requests in [[The Garden]] while [[Finnegan]]'s Blooming Business perk is active rewards the player with an amount of Fine Flour determined by the rarity of the visitor.
|Rarity|Reward|
|-|-|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|1|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|3|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|5|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|10|
Additionally, certain visitors grant extra base Fine Flour. These also get the extra amounts listed above if served while the perk is active.
|Visitor|Base Amount|Total Amount|
|-|-|-|
|[[Pest Wrangler]]|5|6|
|[[Pest Wrangler?]]|50|55|
|[[Vinyl Collector]]|15|18|
## Usage
The player can bring [[Fine Flour]] 3,000 (46 stacks plus 56) Fine Flour to [[Carrolyn]] in [[Scarleton]] to gain a permanent +5 [[Health]] and +12 [[Crop Fortune|Wheat Fortune]], and unlock the [[Baker]] Garden visitor.
Fine Flour can also be used in the [[Composter]] to grant 150 Organic Matter.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Not Very Moldy Bread]]|None|[[Fine Flour]] 16x Fine Flour 32x [[Cornucopia]]|Recipe: [[Cornucopia]] x4 ×8 + Fine Flour x16 → [[Not Very Moldy Bread]]|
## Trivia
- When it was usable in the [[Sprayonator]], it granted 20 [[Farming Fortune]] on top of the 50 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] granted by the Sprayonator. Unlike other materials, it did not favor specific pests.

Finn's Focaccia is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
Finn's Focaccia can be purchased from [[Feast Baker Scott]] for 50 [[Kernels]].
## Usage
Finn's Focaccia can be consumed during a [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]] to gain the Focaccia Fanatic effect which grants +5 [[Overbloom]]. Its effects last for the duration of the [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]] and expires when the event ends.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Consuming Finn's Focaccia|(none)|

# Firefly in the Hole Vinyl
Aliases: Firefly in The Hole Vinyl
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops
[Firefly in the Hole Vinyl]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

Sacks: [[Vinyl Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VINYL_FIREFLY
Tooltip: Place in a Chirping Stereo to play funky tunes for  Pests.; When playing, this vinyl attracts Firefly Pests.; UNCOMMON
A Firefly in the Hole Vinyl is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Firefly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|5%* ([[Pests]])|
## Usage
### Vacuum
The Firefly in the Hole Vinyl can be used in a [[InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius]] to increase the chance of [[Firefly|Fireflies]] spawning.
### Disco Destination
The Firefly in the Hole Vinyl can be used in a [[Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination]] to make [[Firefly|Fireflies]] spawn near it while offline.
### Vinyl Set
The Firefly in the Hole Vinyl can be used in a [[Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set]] to fill it so it can be used to charm [[Garden Visitors]].
## Trivia
- Firefly in the Hole Vinyl is a reference to the phrase "Fire in the hole", a warning often shouted before demolition or an explosion. It was popularized in 2024 throughout memes in the Geometry Dash community.

The Flaming Spider Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Arthropod Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Flaming Spider Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Flaming Spider]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Huntraps
A Flaming Spider Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Burning Desert]].
### Fusion
A Flaming Spider Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Flaming Spider Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Arthropod Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Arthropod Ruler ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3%–30% more [[Damage]] against [[Arthropod]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Flaming Spider Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Flaming Spider Shard has the following description:
- Their glowing eyes and burning fangs make them a nightmarish sight.
## Trivia
- Despite being an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Flaming Spider Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.

Sword:
Flaming Sword [[Damage]]: +50 [[Strength]]: +20

## Trivia
- The Flaming Sword used to have a 3% chance to be obtained via the [[Pigman]] in the [[Blazing Fortress]] before it was replaced by the Crimson Isle, rendering it unobtainable for a year.
- It used to turn into a [[Leaping Sword]] at random times, but could not be auctioned during that period. This was patched, though.

Flares are a sub-class of [[Deployables]].
## Mechanics
Flares can be shot into the sky for 3m and buff up to 8 players in a 40 block radius. Only one [[Deployables|Deployable]] buff applies.
### Upgrading
|Slot|Upgrade|Effect|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Recombobulator 3000]]|[[Recombobulator 3000]]|Can be used in a [[Anvil]] or at the Dungeon Blacksmith ([[Malik]]) to permanently increase the rarity of a Flare by +1.|An item's rarity can only be upgraded once!|
|[[Mana Disintegrator]]|[[Mana Disintegrator]]|Can be combined in an [[Anvil]] with a Flare to reduce their [[Mana]] cost by 1%.|A [[Mana Disintegrator]] may be applied up to 10 times!|
|[[Jalapeno Book]]|[[Jalapeno Book]]|Can be combined in an [[Anvil]] with a Flare to gain a small but permanent stat boost of +5 [[Crit Damage]] and +1% [[Crit Chance]].|A [[Jalapeno Book]] may only be applied once!|

# Flexbone

[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FLEXBONE
Tooltip: Caught as a Treasure catch in the Tomb Floodway on the Moonglade Marsh.; These softened bones have absorbed moss over time, offering a flexible material.; Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
A Flexbone is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that can be fished up in the [[Tomb Floodway]].
## Obtaining
### Fishing
A Flexbone has a 25% chance to replace [[Treasure]] that's caught while [[Fishing]] in the [[Tomb Floodway]]. The amount gained depends on the tier of [[Treasure]] fished up:
|Tier|Amount|
|-|-|
|GOOD CATCH|1x|
|GREAT CATCH|2-4x|
|OUTSTANDING CATCH|8x|
The [[Attributes|Flexible Fisher]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], obtained from syphoning [[Osedax Shard|Osedax Shards]], increases the chances of catching Flexbones by +2.5-25%.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Medium Fishing Net]]|Sea Lumies V (5) ([[Sea Lumies]])|1x [[Basic Fishing Net]] [[Flexbone]] 8x Flexbone 8x [[Enchanted Sea Lumies]] 32x [[Sturdy Bone]]|Recipe: Flexbone x4 ×2 + [[Enchanted Sea Lumies]] x2 ×4 + [[Basic Fishing Net]] + [[Sturdy Bone]] x16 ×2 → [[Medium Fishing Net]]|
|[[Turbo Fishing Net]]|Sea Lumies IX (9) ([[Sea Lumies]])|1x [[Medium Fishing Net]] [[Flexbone]] 32x Flexbone 32x [[Enchanted Sea Lumies]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Sturdy Bone]]|Recipe: Flexbone x16 ×2 + [[Enchanted Sea Lumies]] x8 ×4 + [[Medium Fishing Net]] + [[Sturdy Bone]] x64 ×2 → [[Turbo Fishing Net]]|
|[[Helix Chopper]]|Helix Log VI (6) ([[Helix Log]])|1x [[Figstone Splitter]] 3x [[Helixis]] [[Flexbone]] 8x Flexbone|Recipe: [[Helixis]] ×3 + [[Figstone Splitter]] + Flexbone x8 → [[Helix Chopper]]|
|[[Gigantic Fishing Net]]|Sea Lumies X (10) ([[Sea Lumies]])|1x [[Reinforced Netting]] 1x [[Turbo Fishing Net]] 32x [[Sublime Silk]] [[Flexbone]] 64x Flexbone 128x [[Enchanted Sea Lumies]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Sea Lumies]] x32 ×4 + [[Reinforced Netting]] + [[Sublime Silk]] x16 ×2 + [[Turbo Fishing Net]] + Flexbone x64 → [[Gigantic Fishing Net]]|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 5, 2025|Added Flexbone.|
|Jul 31, 2025|Added Flexbone to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Jul 22, 2026|Changed Flexbone to have a 25% chance to replace fished [[Treasure]], and increased the amount of Flexbones given based on the rarity of the treasure catch.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Flexbone.|
|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Added Flexbone to the [[Bazaar]].|

# Floral Gelatin
Aliases: Gelatin
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Floral Gelatin]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Garden Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FLORAL_GELATIN
Tooltip: Dropped rarely from Wild Rose during the Harvest Feast event.; Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
Floral Gelatin is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item dropped during the [[Harvest Feast]] used to craft [[Royal Jelly]].
## Obtaining
### Farming
Floral Gelatin has a 0.006% (1 in 18,000 crops) chance to drop when breaking mature [[Wild Rose]], a 15% chance to drop from a [[Praying Mantis]] and a 30% chance to drop from a [[Field Mouse]] while the [[Harvest Feast]] is ongoing and the crop is in season.
[[Overbloom]] is used to increase the drop chance.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Royal Jelly]]|None|[[Floral Gelatin]] 64x Floral Gelatin 64x [[Jelly]]|Recipe: Floral Gelatin x8 ×8 + [[Jelly]] x64 → [[Royal Jelly]]|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) [[Orchid Mantis Pet]]|None|1x [[Enchanted Egg]] [[Floral Gelatin]] 8x Floral Gelatin|Recipe: Floral Gelatin ×8 + [[Enchanted Egg]] → [[Orchid Mantis Pet]]|
### [[The Garden|Garden Visitors]]
When visiting [[The Garden]], [[Taylor]] can request [[Floral Gelatin]] 256 (4 stacks) Floral Gelatin in exchange for the [[Greenhouse Skins|Jelly Garden Greenhouse Skin]].
### Orchid Mantis Pet Upgrade
Upgrading [[Orchid Mantis Pet]] at [[Kat]] requires Floral Gelatin.
|Rarity|Ingredients|
|-|-|
|C ([[Rarity]]) → U ([[Rarity]])|[[Floral Gelatin]] 16x Floral Gelatin|
|U ([[Rarity]]) → R ([[Rarity]])|[[Floral Gelatin]] 32x Floral Gelatin|
|R ([[Rarity]]) → E ([[Rarity]])|[[Floral Gelatin]] 48x Floral Gelatin|
|E ([[Rarity]]) → L ([[Rarity]])|[[Floral Gelatin]] 64x Floral Gelatin|
It takes [[Floral Gelatin]] 168 (2 stacks plus 40) Floral Gelatin in total to make a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Orchid Mantis Pet]].
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Added Floral Gelatin.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Changed NPC Sell Price from 5,000 [[Coins]] to 50,000 [[Coins]].|

# Frog's Spirit
Aliases: Leapfrog
Categories: Uncommon items, Items
[Frog's Spirit]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Rosemary]]
Buy: 5,000 (78 stacks plus 8) [[Forest Essence]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FROGS_SPIRIT_SCROLL
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Leapfrog Ability when combined with Canopy Sandals, Fig Striders, or Helix Boots.; Ability: Leapfrog DOUBLE TAP JUMP Grants you the abilities of a Frog while on Foraging Islands, allowing you to Double Jump.; Mana Cost: 25; UNCOMMON ABILITY SCROLL
A Frog's Spirit is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Ability Scrolls|Ability Scroll]].
## Obtaining
Frog's Spirit can be purchased from the [[Essence Shops|Forest Essence Shop]] for 5,000 [[Forest Essence]].
## Usage
A Frog's Spirit can be applied to [[Canopy Armor|Canopy Sandals]], [[Fig Armor|Fig Striders]], or [[Helix Armor|Helix Boots]] in an anvil to give them the Leapfrog ability.
Leapfrog allows the player to double jump by pressing jump in midair. Double jumps can be used repeatably without landing to fly. Using Leapfrog costs 25 [[Mana]]. Using Leapfrog also plays a croaking sound (entity.frog.ambient) and spawns a ring of green particles.
After being applied, a Frog's Spirit can be removed using [[The Hex]]. Player's will not receive a refund and the Frog's Spirit will be destroyed.
## Game Message
|Condition|Guaranteed Message|
|-|-|
|Attempting to use Leapfrog while not on a Foraging Island|You can only use the Leapfrog ability while on Foraging Islands!|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Aug 25, 2026|Added Frog's Spirit.|
|Sep 21, 2026|Added ability to remove Frog's Spirit using [[The Hex]].|
|Sep 21, 2026|Added croaking sound and adjusted particles.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Added Frog's Spirit.|

# Frogcoin
Aliases: Frog Coin, Frog coin
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Fishing
[Frogcoin]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]]); Medium Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]]); Large Fishing Sack ([[Fishing Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FROGCOIN
Tooltip: Throw this into the waters of the Lotus Eater's Cave to gain temporary stat bonuses while on the Lotus Atoll!; A small coin depicting a wrinkly, bearded frog. Coins of this nature are said to bring good luck when thrown into small fountains or ponds.; UNCOMMON
Frogcoin is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv55]|[[Lotus Guardian]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|5%|
## Usage
Frogcoins can be thrown in and donated to the [[Lotus Eater's Cave]], granting 1 randomly chosen from the buffs, unless they're already active, for 30 minutes, while on the [[Lotus Atoll]]:
- Random Rewards
- +1 [[Treasure Chance]]
- +5 [[Trophy Chance]]
- +2.5 [[Sea Creature Chance]]
- +10 [[Fishing Speed]]
While under any of these effects, the players will be able to catch the [[Trophy Frogs|Blessed Frog]] Trophy Frog. Once a fourth one is thrown, the [[The Lotus Eater]] NPC will appear. Throwing multiple Frogcoins at the same time will only consume one of them, while the others remain in the water. Attempting to throw a fifth Frogcoin does nothing.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Lotus Water Orb]]|None|1x [[Water Orb]] 8x [[Unlucky Doubloon]] 8x [[EroC enotS egrofeR]] 16x [[Gold Lotus]] [[Frogcoin]] 32x Frogcoin|Recipe: Frogcoin x8 ×4 + [[EroC enotS egrofeR]] x8 + [[Gold Lotus]] x8 ×2 + [[Water Orb]] + [[Unlucky Doubloon]] x8 → [[Lotus Water Orb]]|
|[[Geometric Oddity]]|None|1x [[EroC enotS egrofeR]] [[Frogcoin]] 8x Frogcoin 16x [[Rusty Coin]] 32x [[Ink Sac]] 64x [[Treasure Bait]]|Recipe: [[Ink Sac]] x32 + [[Rusty Coin]] x16 + [[EroC enotS egrofeR]] + Frogcoin x8 + [[Treasure Bait]] x64 → [[Geometric Oddity]]|
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Throwing the first to third coins|WISE! You've been granted [stat] for 30m while on the Lotus Atoll!|
|Throwing the fourth coin|WISE! You've been granted [stat] for 30m while on the Lotus Atoll! The Lotus Eater beckons you from his cave...|
|If all the buffs are already active|The waters have denied your Frogcoin, offering nothing in return! But The Lotus Eater awaits in his cave...|

# Garden Scythe
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Tools, Farming, Farming tools
[Garden Scythe]
Type: Tool
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

[Shop]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GARDEN_SCYTHE
Tooltip: Cleans a 5x5 area of grass, leaves and flowers.; UNCOMMON
The Garden Scythe is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Hoes|Hoe]] that clears a 5x5 area of [[Grass]], [[Flowers]] and [[Leaves]].
## Obtaining
Garden Scythe can be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for [[Sam's Scythe]] and 20 [[Copper]].
## Usage
Upon usage, the Garden Scythe will automatically clear the blocks in a 5x5 area. It will clear [[Leaves]], [[Flowers]], [[Grass]] and [[Fern]].
## Trivia
- Despite having "Scythe" in its name, it is not classified as one.

The Gazer Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Zealot Tracker]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Gazer Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Gazer]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
### Fusion
A Gazer Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||

[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Gazer Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Zealot Tracker]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Zealot Tracker ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Tracking]] towards Special Zealots ([[Special Zealot]]).
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Gazer Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Gazer Shard has the following description:
- Nothing is known about the Gazer, save for the fact that it detests light.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LARGE_GEMSTONE_SACK
Tooltip: Holds Gemstones of all types and tiers! Items you pickup go directly into your sacks.; Capacity: 128,000 per gemstone type Multiple sacks sum their capacity!; Right Click to open sack!; UNCOMMON SACK
Gemstone Sack is a [[Sacks|Sack]] unlocked at [[Gemstone|Gemstone I (1)]]. It has 3 sizes, each size increasing the number of items it can hold. The amount of each item the sack can hold is determined by the tier's weight, with Rough being 1, Flawed being 80, and Fine being 6,400. The Small Gemstone Sack can hold 128,000 weight, the Medium Gemstone Sack can hold 512,000 weight, and the Large Gemstone Sack can hold 2,560,000 weight.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Small Gemstone Sack ([[Gemstone Sack]])|Gemstone I (1) ([[Gemstone]])|1x [[Enchanted Leather]] 80x Rough Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|Recipe: [[Gemstone]] x10 ×8 + [[Enchanted Leather]] → [[Gemstone Sack]]|
|Medium Gemstone Sack ([[Gemstone Sack]])|Gemstone III (3) ([[Gemstone]])|1x Small Gemstone Sack ([[Gemstone Sack]]) 80x Flawed Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|Recipe: [[Gemstone]] x10 ×8 + [[Gemstone Sack]] → [[Gemstone Sack]]|
|Large Gemstone Sack ([[Gemstone Sack]])|Gemstone VI (6) ([[Gemstone]])|1x Medium Gemstone Sack ([[Gemstone Sack]]) 8x Fine Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|Recipe: [[Gemstone]] ×8 + [[Gemstone Sack]] → [[Gemstone Sack]]|
## Usage
The Gemstone Sack can store [[Gemstone|Gemstones]].
|Sack|Capacity|Items|
|-|-|-|
|Small Gemstone Sack ([[Gemstone Sack]])|128,000|Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Flawed Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Fine Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Rough Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Flawed Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Fine Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Rough Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Flawed Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Fine Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Rough Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Flawed Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Fine Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Rough Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Flawed Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Fine Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Rough Jasper Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Flawed Jasper Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Fine Jasper Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Rough Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Flawed Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Fine Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Rough Opal Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Flawed Opal Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Fine Opal Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Rough Onyx Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Flawed Onyx Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Fine Onyx Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Rough Aquamarine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Flawed Aquamarine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Fine Aquamarine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Rough Citrine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Flawed Citrine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Fine Citrine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Rough Peridot Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Flawed Peridot Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]), Fine Peridot Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|
|Medium Gemstone Sack ([[Gemstone Sack]])|512,000|(same as above)|
|Large Gemstone Sack ([[Gemstone Sack]])|2,560,000|(same as above)|
## Trivia
- While both small and medium variants have a collection requirement to unlock the crafting recipe, the large variant doesn't.
- For a short time after the v0.12 Crystal Hollows Update, Extra Large Gemstone Sack (EXTRA_LARGE_GEMSTONE_SACK) was craftable with 42 [[Gemstone|Rough Ruby Gemstone]], 16 [[Enchanted String]] and 4 [[Enchanted Leather]]. It did not function as a Sack, and was placeable. Its recipe was removed soon after.
- Ever since a patch made it so flawless and perfect gemstones could not be stacked, these variants are not able to be placed in gemstone sacks.
## Gallery
- The Extra Large Gemstone Sack that has been removed.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Gemstone Sack.|
|Mar 8, 2022|Added Opal Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]) to the Gemstone Sack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Gemstone Sack.|
|Jul 16, 2021|Removed recipe for the Extra Large Gemstone Sack.|
|Sep 13, 2021|Fixed Full Inventory message spam.|
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Opal Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]) to the Gemstone Sack.|
|May 11, 2023|0.18.4|Small Gemstone Sack is now UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]).|
|Jul 13, 2026|Fixed the Filter options being in the wrong order in the Gemstone Sack|

The Glacial Ring is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that grants +10 [[Mining Wisdom]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]]. It is the upgraded form of the [[Glacial Talisman]] and can be further upgraded to the [[Glacial Artifact]].

The Glowing Mushroom is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item obtained from the [[Glowing Mushroom Cave]].
## Obtaining
Glowing Mushrooms can be obtained by breaking [[Mushroom|Mushrooms]] with particles above them in the [[Glowing Mushroom Cave]]. An extra 1 Glowing Mushroom can be obtained when breaking a mushroom by using [[Moby's Shears]].
## Usage
Glowing Mushrooms can be used to purchase items from [[Moby]].

|[[Luminous Bracelet]]|[[Luminous Bracelet]]|2x Full Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]]) [[Glowing Mushroom]] 10x Glowing Mushroom|
|[[Chum Sinker]]|[[Chum Sinker]]|4x Full Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]]) [[Glowing Mushroom]] 15x Glowing Mushroom|
|[[Moby's Shears]]|[[Moby's Shears]]|5x Full Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]]) [[Glowing Mushroom]] 15x Glowing Mushroom|
|[[Mushed Glowy Tonic Potion]]|[[Mushed Glowy Tonic Potion]]|8x Full Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]]) [[Glowing Mushroom]] 10x Glowing Mushroom|
|[[Glowing Mush Mixin]]|[[Glowing Mush Mixin]]|64x Full Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]]) [[Glowing Mushroom]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Glowing Mushroom|
|[[Travel Scroll to the Glowing Mushroom Cave]]|[[Travel Scroll to the Glowing Mushroom Cave]]|64x Full Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]]) [[Glowing Mushroom]] 32x Glowing Mushroom|
## Gallery
- A Red Glowing Mushroom in the Glowing Mushroom Cave A Red [[Glowing Mushroom]] Glowing Mushroom in the [[Glowing Mushroom Cave]]
## Trivia
- Glowing Mushrooms are not affected by [[Farming Fortune]]. The only way to increase their drop rate is to use [[Moby's Shears]].
- Red and Brown [[Glowing Mushroom]] Glowing Mushrooms will produce different-colored particle effects in block form. These colors are:
  - Red: White, Red, Green, and rarely Orange
  - Brown: White, Light Tan, Green, and rarely Orange

# Glowy Chum Bait
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Fishing
[Glowy Chum Bait]
Type: [[Bait]]
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Medium Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Large Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Moby]]
Buy: 4x Glowing Mushrooms ([[Glowing Mushroom]]) 1x Full Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]])
Sell: 25 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GLOWY_CHUM_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Grants +25 Fishing Speed.; Grants +3 Sea Creature Chance.; Adds +2 Chums dropped on Sea Creatures fished with this bait.; UNCOMMON BAIT
Glowy Chum Bait is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Bait]].
## Obtaining
16x Glowy Chum Bait can be purchased from [[Moby]] for 4 [[Glowing Mushroom|Glowing Mushrooms]] and 1 [[Chum Bucket|Full Chum Bucket]].
Glowy Chum Bait may also have a 0.72% chance to appear as [[Glacial Cave|Frozen Treasure]].
## Usage
### Fishing
Glowy Chum Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Glowy Chum Bait will be consumed to grant +25 [[Fishing Speed]], +3 [[Sea Creature Chance]] and +2 additional [[Chum|Chums]] from killed [[Sea Creatures]]. Note that the [[Fishing Speed]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]] don't show up in the player's stats. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.

Golden Bait is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Bait]].
## Obtaining
Golden Bait can be fished while using [[Rarity|RARE]] and above [[Seal Pet]] during [[Year of the Seal]].
### Seal's Treat Bag
128x Golden Bait have a 5.5% (1 in 18) chance to be obtained from a [[Seal's Treat Bag]].
### Seal Pet
The [[Rarity|RARE]], [[Rarity|EPIC]], and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Seal Pet]]'s Peak Performance has a 0.035% (7 in 20000)–5% (1 in 20) chance to materialize [[Golden Bait]] Golden Bait in the player's inventory upon catching [[Treasure]] during the [[Year of the Seal]].
## Usage
### Fishing
Golden Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Golden Bait will be consumed to grant +4 [[Treasure Chance]]. Note that the [[Treasure Chance]] doesn't show up in the player's stats. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GRAND_EXP_BOTTLE
Tooltip: Smash it open to receive experience!; UNCOMMON
Grand Experience Bottle is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item and the second tier Experience Bottle. It grants a base of 1,500 [[Experience]] at [[Enchanting|Enchanting 0]], up to 6,000 [[Experience]] at [[Enchanting|Enchanting LX (60)]] without other buffs.
## Obtaining
Grand Experience Bottles can be crafted, fished up, looted from [[Ender Nodes]], or obtained from the [[Experimentation Table]].
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Lapis Lazuli V (5) ([[Lapis Lazuli]])|1x [[Glass Bottle]] 6x [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli]] ×6 + [[Glass Bottle]] → Grand Experience Bottle|
### [[Fishing]]
Grand Experience Bottles can be caught as a GOOD CATCH while fishing in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] with a weight of 50, [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] with a weight of 15, or on the [[Crimson Isle]] with the weight of 5.
### [[Ender Nodes]]
Up to [[Grand Experience Bottle]] 5 Grand Experience Bottles at a time can be obtained by mining an Ender Node.
- [[End Stone]]
  - [[Grand Experience Bottle]] 1 Grand Experience Bottle (11.39% chance)
- [[Obsidian]]
  - [[Grand Experience Bottle]] 2 Grand Experience Bottles (13.96% chance)
  - [[Grand Experience Bottle]] 5 Grand Experience Bottles (6.11% chance)
### [[Experimentation Table]]
Grand Experience Bottles can be obtained by packs of 5, 10 or 20 from Superpairs. They are very common.
### Purchase
Grand Experience Bottles can be bought from the [[Mage Emissary]] in [[Scarleton]] for 5,000 [[Coins]] if the player is part of the [[Factions|Mage Faction]]. A discount of up to 20% is provided depending on the player's [[Factions|ቾ Mage Reputation]].
## Usage
### Joining the [[Rift Dimension]]
By paying [[Grand Experience Bottle]] 32 Grand Experience Bottles, one can get a Rift Entry, bypassing the 4-hour time limit.
### Gain Experience
Note: Players will get varying amounts of experience obtained based upon their enchanting level and other factors. See [[Experience]] for more details.

# Grappling Hook
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Craftable items, String Collection, Tools, Other Tools
[Grappling Hook]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): String IV (4) ([[String]])
Tooltip Text: Travel around in style using this Grappling Hook.
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No
Reforgeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1,200 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 480x (7 stacks plus 32 (480 in total)) [[String]] 2x [[Stick]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: 2 Stick)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GRAPPLING_HOOK
Tooltip: Travel around in style using this Grappling Hook.; 2 Second Cooldown; UNCOMMON
The Grappling Hook is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item. It is unlocked at [[String|String IV (4)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|String IV (4) ([[String]])|2x [[Stick]] 3x [[Enchanted String]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted String]] ×3 + [[Stick]] ×2 → Grappling Hook|
## Usage
The Grappling Hook has the model of a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]]: casting it and reeling will fling the player towards the position of the bobber. It has a 2s cooldown. Using the Grappling Hook while it is on cooldown will display the following message:
Whoa! Slow down there!
The [[Bat Person Armor]]'s Bat Powers Activate! full set bonus removes the cooldown of the Grappling Hook and increases its power and range.
## Trivia
- By combining a [[Grappling Hook]] Grappling Hook with the [[Spider's Boots]]'s Double Jump ability, players can essentially fly.
  - 8[[Ranks|[PIG+++]]] Technoblade demonstrated this in his video "flying in skyblock".
- Due to the Grappling Hook's cheap cost and early game accessibility, some aspects of the game are built with the assumption that the player has access to a Grappling Hook.
  - For example, many [[Fairy Souls]] are located in positions that require the use of mobility items such as the Grappling Hook to access.
- Unlike other mobility items such as [[Aspect of the End]], the Grappling Hook does not reset a player's downward speed, and as such will not prevent fall damage.
- Players can use a Grappling Hook while facing down or up to scale walls up to 5 blocks at a time.
  - Players can scale walls up to 11 blocks with the [[Bat Person Armor]] set bonus.
- Players can jump right before they grapple in order to increase the distance traveled.
- The Grappling Hook also appears as a gadget for Hypixel lobbies.
- As counter-intuitive as it sounds, players wishing to use the Grappling Hook and [[Bat Person Armor]] who wish to cross long distances in the air should consider moving backwards while in the air before reeling their rod in, as the further the distance between the player and the bobber, the faster the player will move when they reel.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Grappling Hook.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Moved the Grappling Hook recipe from String VII (7) ([[String]]) to String IV (4) ([[String]]).|

# Gravity Talisman
Categories: Uncommon items, Accessories, Craftable items, Obsidian Collection, Talisman
[Gravity Talisman]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Obsidian III (3) ([[Obsidian]])
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 864 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 144x (2 stacks plus 16 (144 in total)) [[Obsidian]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GRAVITY_TALISMAN
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +5; Gives between +1 and +10  Strength and  Defense the closer you are to the spawn of an Island!; UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
The Gravity Talisman is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] unlocked at [[Obsidian|Obsidian III (3)]]. It grants between 1 and 10 [[Strength]] and [[Defense]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Obsidian III (3) ([[Obsidian]])|144x [[Obsidian]]|Recipe: [[Obsidian]] x16 ×9 → Gravity Talisman|
## Usage
Accessory:
Gravity Talisman
[Effects]
[Gives between +1 and +10 Strength and Defense the closer you are to the spawn of an island!]
Whilst held in the player's inventory, or inside of the [[Accessory Bag]], the Gravity Talisman will give the player a base increase of +1 [[Strength]], +1 [[Defense]], which then increases to a maximum of +10 [[Strength]], +10 [[Defense]] depending on the player's proximity to the spawn of the island they are standing on. The item uses the island spawn point as the center of the island instead of the actual center of the island.
## Trivia
- The item was released without the ability but instead given a +10 Speed buff.
- On islands with customizable spawn points ([[Private Island]] and [[The Garden]]), it always uses the default spawn point as base.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Gravity Talisman.|
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Fixed bug that prevented the item ability from working.|

The Green Belt is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Belts|Belt]]. It is obtainable from [[Master Tao]] after reaching 2000 points in the [[Dojo]]. It gives +20 [[Health]] and +10 [[Defense]]. Its ability reduces all damage taken by 3%. This item is automatically Soulbound upon receiving it. It can be upgraded into the [[Blue Belt]], and is a direct upgrade from the [[Yellow Belt]].

The Green Elf Jerry Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on December 25, 2020. Being [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 500 purchases.

# Green Gift
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Events, Season of Jerry, Gifts
[Green Gift]
Type: Gift item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Material Tiers]
← Previous: [[White Gift]]
Next →: [[Red Gift]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Winter Sack]]; [[Events Sack]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GREEN_GIFT
Tooltip: Click a stranger while holding to gift!; Both players get the rewards!; UNCOMMON
Green Gift is a [[Gifts|Gift]] obtained from [[Jerry's Workshop]]. A player can give this to another player, allowing both of them to receive rewards.
## Obtaining
### Gift Locations
Every SkyBlock Year, the player can speak to [[St. Jerry]] to obtain a Green Gift after claiming all 20 [[White Gift|White Gifts]] across [[Jerry's Workshop]].
### Frozen Treasure
Within the [[Glacial Cave]], players can mine [[Glacial Cave|Frozen Treasure]] to obtain Green Gifts.
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv3]|Frosty ([[Frosty (mob)]])|0-1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv25]|[[Grinch]]|0-1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
Damaging a [[Reindrake]] also has a 25% chance to drop a Green Gift.
### Season of Jerry
During the [[Season of Jerry]] event, players can earn Gifts in [[Jerry's Workshop]] of all kinds across waves for [[Defend Jerry's Workshop|defending]] the village.
## Usage
### Gifts
A Green Gift can be given to another player to spawn a present in which that player can open, with both players receiving the item inside. Once opened, the item will drop on the ground and only the 2 players that opened and gifted the present will be able to pick up the items.
[[Snowman Mask]], [[White Gift Talisman]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] line, [[Snowfin Shard]] and the Professional Gifter perk from [[Doug]] give more rewards from [[Gifts]]. Green Gifts also have a 30% chance to give the player 1–3 [[North Stars]].

# Green Goblin Egg
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crystal Hollows
[Green Goblin Egg]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: What came first the goblin or the egg?
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Dwarven Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GOBLIN_EGG_GREEN
Tooltip: What came first the goblin or the egg?; UNCOMMON
Green Goblin Egg is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item used to forge the [[Pesto Goblin Omelette]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv89]|[[Creeperlobber]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.25%|
|[Lv65]|[[Goblin Flamethrower]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.25%|
|[Lv96]|[[Murderlover]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.25%|
### Treasure Chests
Green Goblin Eggs can be dropped from chests in the [[Goblin Holdout]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. The chests can either be found in naturally spawning structures like the [[Goblin Queen's Den]] and by [[King Yolkar]] or can be spawned by the player while mining [[Hard Stone]] in the [[Goblin Holdout]].
### [[Frozen Corpses]]
Goblin Eggs can be obtained from opening [[Frozen Corpses]].
|Type|Quantity|Chance Per Roll|
|-|-|-|
|Lapis|[[Green Goblin Egg]] 1 Green Goblin Egg|4.18% (1 in 23.93)|
|Lapis|[[Green Goblin Egg]] 2 Green Goblin Egg|2.09% (1 in 47.86)|
|Lapis|[[Green Goblin Egg]] 4 Green Goblin Egg|1.04% (1 in 95.71)|
## Usage
### Spawning Golden Goblins
Throwing a Green Goblin Egg in [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Glacite Mineshafts]] or [[Glacite Tunnels]] has there's a 2.5% (1 in 40 eggs) chance to spawn a [[Golden Goblin]], with a base 3.33% chance to be replaced with [[Diamond Goblin]].
### Forging
#### [[Pesto Goblin Omelette]]
- 1 [[Pesto Goblin Omelette]]
  - [[Green Goblin Egg]] 96 (1 stack plus 32) Green Goblin Egg
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Jade Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Jade Gemstone]] 6,400 (100 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Jade Gemstone]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Jade Gemstone]]
  - 1 [[Goblin Omelette]] 96 [[Goblin Egg]]
### Fuel
The Green Goblin Egg can be used as a [[Machine Fuel]], adding 5,000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]].

The Green Jerry Talisman is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] found in [[Jerry Box|Jerry Boxes]]. It grants the user -1 [[Intelligence]]. It can be upgraded to the [[Blue Jerry Talisman]] and further upgraded to the [[Purple Jerry Talisman]] from where it can finally be upgraded to the [[Golden Jerry Artifact]].
## Obtaining
The Green Jerry Talisman is found inside the [[Jerry Box]] with a 6.61% from the Green and Blue Jerry Box and a 6.47% from the Purple Jerry Box.
It is not obtainable from the Golden Jerry Box.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Blue Jerry Talisman]]|None|[[Green Jerry Talisman]] 5x Green Jerry Talisman|Recipe: Green Jerry Talisman ×5 → [[Blue Jerry Talisman]]|
## Trivia
- While its debuff of -1 [[Intelligence]] might sound unattractive at first, it still grants [[Accessory Power]].
- If the player is manually farming [[Hidden Jerry|Hidden Jerries]], having [[Tracking]] reduces the chance of obtaining Green Jerry Talismans due to [[Jerry Box|Golden Jerry Boxes]] not having them in the loot pool.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 11, 2021|Added Green Jerry Talisman.|

[Shop]
Merchants: [[Zog]]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_ITEM_HARDENED_SCALES_UNCOMMON
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Grants +25 Defense.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; UNCOMMON PET ITEM
Hardened Scales are an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]]. It grants +25 [[Defense]] when applied to [[Pets]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
Hardened Scales can be purchased from [[Zog]] in the [[Village]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Hardened Scales will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +25 [[Defense]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Hardened Scales for 50,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|

# Hardened Wood
Aliases: Stiff
Categories: Uncommon items, Reforge stones
[Hardened Wood]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Stiff
Applied To: [[Fishing Rods]]
Apply Cost: 25,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Jake]]
Buy: 15,000 [[Coins]]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HARDENED_WOOD
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Stiff reforge when combined with a Fishing Rod.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Strength  Sea Creature Chance; Stiff Bonus Sea Creatures killed with this Fishing Rod drop +1 Chum.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 12.; UNCOMMON REFORGE STONE
Hardened Wood is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Stiff]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], which grants [[Strength]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]].
## Obtaining
Hardened Wood is sold by [[Jake]] for 15,000 [[Coins]] after bringing him an [[Oasis Sheep]] to [[Jake's House|his house]] on the mountain.
## Usage
### Reforging
Stiff Wood can be applied to [[Fishing Rods]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Stiff]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]], which scale with the Fishing Rod's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XII (12)]]. A Fishing Rod that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Stiff]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Stiff Bonus, which grants +1 [[Chum]] when killing a [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] using a Fishing Rod with this reforge.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+1 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +2 Str ([[Strength]])|+2 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +4 Str ([[Strength]])|+2 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +6 Str ([[Strength]])|+3 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +8 Str ([[Strength]])|+5 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +10 Str ([[Strength]])|+7 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +12 Str ([[Strength]])|+9 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +14 Str ([[Strength]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Stiff Bonus; Sea Creatures killed with this Fishing Rod drop +1 Chum.|Stiff Bonus; Sea Creatures killed with this Fishing Rod drop +1 Chum.|Stiff Bonus; Sea Creatures killed with this Fishing Rod drop +1 Chum.|Stiff Bonus; Sea Creatures killed with this Fishing Rod drop +1 Chum.|Stiff Bonus; Sea Creatures killed with this Fishing Rod drop +1 Chum.|Stiff Bonus; Sea Creatures killed with this Fishing Rod drop +1 Chum.|Stiff Bonus; Sea Creatures killed with this Fishing Rod drop +1 Chum.|
|Cost to apply|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|
## Bugs
- As of SkyBlock 0.25, Hardened Wood is difficult to obtain without trading with players due to [[Oasis Sheep]] despawning before reaching [[Jake]].
  - One known method to obtain it is to [[Cocooning|Cocoon]] the [[Oasis Sheep]] midway through to the mountain before it despawns.
## Trivia
- Before 0.27.2 Hardened Wood's texture resembled the texture of Stripped Spruce Wood in vanilla Minecraft.

## Obtaining
### Crafting

|-|-|-|
|End Stone VI (6) ([[End Stone]])|1x [[Enchanted Feather]] 8x [[Enchanted End Stone]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted End Stone]] ×8 + [[Enchanted Feather]] → Haste Block x8|

The Haste Block grants +100 [[Speed]] when standing on the block.

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Hawk Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Helix Collector]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Helix Collector ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Helix Fortune]] for every digit in your Helix Log collection (Max 10M collection).
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

### Shop Purchase
A Heron Shard can be purchased from [[Agatha]] for 15 [[Agatha's Coupon]] after reaching [[Agatha|Agatha Milestone IV]].
### Fusion
A Heron Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Heron Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Mangrove Sharpening]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Mangrove Sharpening ([[Attributes]]); Grants +10–100 [[Sweep]] towards Mangrove Trees.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Hoe of No Tilling
Aliases: Hoe Of No Tilling
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Tools
Mathematically proven to be more efficient than jumping on it!
[Hoe of No Tilling]
Type: Tool
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HOE_OF_NO_TILLING
Tooltip: Untills farmland!; Mathematically proven to be more efficient than jumping on it!; This item can be reforged!; UNCOMMON
Hoe of No Tilling is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item. It displays the buyer's name, cost paid, and the details about in which auction it was sold and the ranking of the buyer within that auction. This data disappears if the item is traded in any way.
## Obtaining
Hoe of No Tilling is one of the five items that appear at [[Shen's Auction|Shen's Special]] while [[Diaz]] is elected with her Volume Trading perk and can be bid on by players. The top 20 bidders win the item. [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] profiles have a separate auction, where top 10 bidders get the item.
## Usage
Hoe:
Hoe of No Tilling
[Effects]
[Untills farmland!]
When right-clicking [[Farmland]] on [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden|Garden]], it turns the block into [[Dirt]], regardless of if it was dirt before being tilled or not.
## Trivia
- Hoe of No Tilling was originally suggested by 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Arti_Creep. The original suggestion features the rarity and the description including the quote, but it was originally a [[Diamond Hoe]].

# Honey Jar
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops
[Honey Jar]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Garden Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 100 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HONEY_JAR
Tooltip: Can be spread across a Garden plot with a Sprayonator or used in Pest Traps.; Attracts: Moth, Cricket; UNCOMMON
A Honey Jar is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that can be used with a [[Sprayonator]] to attract [[Pests]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Field Mouse]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv1]|[[Beetle]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Cricket]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Dragonfly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Earthworm]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Firefly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Fly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Locust]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Mite]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Mosquito]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Moth]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Praying Mantis]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Rat]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Slug]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
### Farming
A Honey Jar can also be obtained from the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Bee Pet]]'s Honey Harvester perk, up to a 0.02% (1 in 5000) chance when farming crops.
### Attributes
[[Attributes|Visitor Honey]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants up to 2.5% chance for [[Garden Visitors|Visitors]] to bring Honey Jar, which can be further increased with [[Attributes|Queenly Echo]] attribute.
[[Attributes|Sprayonator Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants 10% chance for [[Pests]] to drop an extra [[Sprayonator|Sprayonator Material]].
## Usage
A Honey Jar can be used with a [[Sprayonator]] to attract [[Moth|Moths]] and [[Cricket|Crickets]] to a sprayed plot.
A Honey Jar can also be used as bait in a [[Pest Trap]], [[Mouse Trap]] or [[Vermin Trap]], allowing them to catch [[Pests]], and increasing the chance to catch a [[Cricket]] or a [[Moth]] by 12x.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Nov 8, 2023|Added Honey Jar.|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Honey Jar as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
|Jul 15, 2026|Honey Jar can now be obtained from the [[Bee Pet]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Nov 14, 2023|0.19.7|Added Honey Jar.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Honey Jar as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Honey Jar can now be obtained from the [[Bee Pet]].|

The Honeymite Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Mite Spawner]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Honeymite Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Honeymite]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Honeymite Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Honeymite Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Mite Spawner]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Mite Spawner ([[Attributes]]); Grants a +2.5%–25% chance to spawn an additional [[Nest Endermite]] when mining [[Ender Nodes]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Honeymite Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Honeymite Shard has the following description:
- Honeymites burrow inside of Beehives, consuming all of the honey, and the colony, from the inside-out.

# Hot Bait
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Craftable items, Fishing
[Hot Bait]
Type: [[Bait]]
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Medium Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]]); Large Bait Sack ([[Bait Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 30 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HOT_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Grants +15 Fishing Speed and +5 Trophy Chance.; UNCOMMON BAIT
Hot Bait is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Bait]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Magmafish II (2) ([[Magmafish]])|1x [[Blaze Ashes]] 1x [[Magmafish]]|Recipe: [[Blaze Ashes]] + [[Magmafish]] → Hot Bait x16|
### Seal's Treat Bag
128x Hot Bait have a 5.5% (1 in 18) chance to be obtained from a [[Seal's Treat Bag]].
## Usage
### Fishing
Hot Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Hot Bait will be consumed to grant +5 [[Trophy Chance]] and +15 [[Fishing Speed]]. Note that the [[Fishing Speed]] and [[Trophy Chance]] don't show up in the player's stats. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.

Hot Chocolate is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that grants the Obsidian Skin effect.
## Obtaining
Hot Chocolate can be bought from [[Grog]] in [[Dragontail]] or [[Ezekiel]] in [[Scarleton]] for 70,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Hot Chocolate can be consumed to gain the Obsidian Skin effect for 2 minutes. This causes the player to not instantly die while touching the [[Blazing Volcano]]'s lava during an [[Blazing Volcano|Eruption]], and instead take normal damage.
## Trivia
- The [[Hot Chocolate|Obsidian Skin]] effect references the Terraria potion effect of the same name.

The Hunter Knife is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Swords|Sword]].
## Obtaining
The Hunter Knife is received after the player speaks to [[Gustave]] after finishing the [[Woods Race]] in 1 minute or less. Afterwards, it can be purchased from [[Rusty]] for 500,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Weapon
Sword:
Hunter Knife [[Damage]]: +50 [[Speed]]: +40
### Minion Upgrade
Hunter Knife can be placed in a [[Potato Minion]]. It makes the minion produce [[French Fries]].
## Tips
- It's good for early-game grinding, such as slaying Miner Zombies for [[Miner Armor]] as it gives a [[Speed]] boost.
- Although this weapon is not very effective for damage, the +40 [[Speed]] could be quite useful early-game before using [[God Potion]], especially when combined with the [[Rogue Sword]].
## Trivia
- It is the only weapon obtained from a [[Island Racing|Race]].

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: HYPER_FURNACE
Tooltip: Smelts items instantly.; UNCOMMON
Hyper Furnace is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] utility block unlocked at [[Cobblestone|Cobblestone VIII (8)]]. It is an upgrade from the regular [[Furnace]], and can smelt and cook items nearly instantly.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Cobblestone VIII (8) ([[Cobblestone]])|8x [[Enchanted Coal]] 64x [[Enchanted Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] x8 ×8 + [[Enchanted Coal]] x8 → Hyper Furnace|
## Mechanics
When using [[Hopper]] setup for a Hyper Furnace, the Hyper Furnace will only be as fast as the Hoppers, which is 0.4 items per second. Based on the assumption that the Hyper Furnace handles one item per tick, that means the Hyper Furnace is 8 times faster than the Hoppers.
### Items smelted per fuel
- Lava Bucket = 2500 items (39.0625 stacks)
- Block of Coal = 2000 items (31.25 stacks)
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 15, 2020|Added Hyper Furnace.|

## Usage
### Fishing
Ice Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], an Ice Bait will be consumed to grant +25 [[Fishing Speed]] and +4 [[Sea Creature Chance]] in the [[Jerry's Workshop]]. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.
## Trivia
- Ice Bait is required to fish up the [[Wet Napkin]].

An Imagine Dragonflies Vinyl is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Dragonfly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|5%* ([[Pests]])|
## Usage
### Vacuum
The Imagine Dragonflies Vinyl can be used in a [[InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius]] to increase the chance of [[Dragonfly|Dragonflies]] spawning.
### Disco Destination
The Imagine Dragonflies Vinyl can be used in a [[Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination]] to make [[Dragonfly|Dragonflies]] spawn near it while offline.
### Vinyl Set
The Imagine Dragonflies Vinyl can be used in a [[Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set]] to fill it so it can be used to charm [[Garden Visitors]].
## Trivia
- Imagine Dragonflies Vinyl is a reference to the band Imagine Dragons.

# InfiniDirt™ Wand
Aliases: Dirt Wand, InfiniDirt Wand, Infinidirt Wand
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Tools, Other Tools
[InfiniDirt™ Wand]
Type: Utility
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Anita]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Anita]]
Buy: 1 [[Jacob's Ticket]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: INFINIDIRT_WAND
Tooltip: Ability: Place Dirt RIGHT CLICK Place a dirt block.; Costs 1 coin.; Can be used within the Builder's Wand!; UNCOMMON
The InfiniDirt™ Wand is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item which allows players to place a [[Dirt]] block, at the cost of 1 [[Coins|Coin]] from their purse. It can also be placed inside the [[Builder's Wand]] and will function as normal dirt would inside it (but still requires 1 [[Coins|Coin]] per block).
## Obtaining
The InfiniDirt™ Wand can be purchased from [[Anita]] for 1 [[Jacob's Ticket]].
## Usage
Item:
InfiniDirt™ Wand
[Effects]
[Ability: Place Dirt RIGHT CLICK; Place a dirt block.; Costs 1 Coin (Coins).; Can be used within the Builder's Wand!]
## Trivia
- The wand has a range of 24 blocks, a significantly larger reach than the player's default reach.
- The wand will only place [[Dirt]] on any side of other solid blocks. This means Dirt will not be placed on blocks like Farmland (Tilled Dirt), Water, Glowstone, and Crops.
- This wand is a very useful tool for any player looking to build farms, as it can be used alongside [[Builder's Wand]], saving lots of time from going to the [[Hub Island]] to buy more dirt. It is very cheap as well so it is definitely recommended to pair this with the Builder's Wand when creating a farm.
- Despite it not being mentioned, it can be used within [[Builder's Ruler]] as well.
- It originally cost 2 [[Coins]] to place each dirt when it was released in early access. This got changed when it was released to everyone.
